                                        The correct answer is 3) A is true, but R is false.

                                        \n\n

                                        Key Points

                                        \n\n
                                          \n
                                        • Assertion (A): The murals in Tirumalaipuram and Sittanavasal caves are important surviving examples of Pandya art. This statement is true.
                                          • \n
                                            \n
                                          • The Sittanavasal cave paintings, located in Tamil Nadu, are one of the finest examples of early Pandya art and culture.
                                            • \n
                                          • These paintings primarily depict scenes of daily life, animals, and lotus ponds, showcasing the artistic skills of the Pandya period (7th century CE).
                                            • \n
                                          • The murals in these caves were created using the fresco-secco technique, which involves painting on dry plaster.
                                            • \n
                                          • Similarly, the Tirumalaipuram caves also hold significant artistic value, with inscriptions and carvings attributed to the Pandya dynasty.
                                            • \n
                                          \n
                                        • Reason", "hn": "\n

                                          सही उत्तर 3) A सत्य है, लेकिन R असत्य है।

                                          \n\n

                                          Key Points

                                          \n\n
                                            \n
                                          • अभिकथन (A): तिरुमलैपुरम और सित्तनवासल गुफाओं के भित्ति चित्र पांड्य कला के महत्वपूर्ण जीवित उदाहरण हैं। यह कथन सत्य है।
                                            • \n
                                              \n
                                            • तमिलनाडु में स्थित सित्तनवासल गुफा चित्र, प्रारंभिक पांड्य कला और संस्कृति के बेहतरीन उदाहरणों में से एक हैं।
                                              • \n
                                            • ये चित्र मुख्य रूप से दैनिक जीवन, जानवरों और कमल के तालाबों के दृश्यों को दर्शाते हैं, जो पांड्य काल (7वीं शताब्दी CE) के कलात्मक कौशल को प्रदर्शित करते हैं।
                                              • \n
                                            • इन गुफाओं में भित्ति चित्र फ्रेस्को-सेक्को तकनीक का उपयोग करके बनाए गए थे, जिसमें सूखे प्लास्टर पर चित्रकारी की जाती है।
                                              • \n
                                            • इसी तरह, तिरुमलैपुरम गुफाओं का भी महत्वपूर्ण कलात्मक मूल्य है, जिसमें पांड्य राजवंश को समर्पित शिलालेख और नक्काशी है।
                                              • \n
                                            \n

In Kho-Kho, the chaser is allowed to change direction in the free zone near the poles, which ensures smooth gameplay and prevents obstruction in the central lane. This rule maintains the integrity of the game and allows the chasers to strategize effectively.
                                                • \n
                                              • A runner is not out even if they step outside the playing area while escaping: This statement is incorrect. In Kho-Kho, runners stepping outside the designated playing area are considered out as they are violating the boundaries of the game.                                               • Kho-Kho Overview: \n
                                                  \n
                                                • Kho-Kho is a traditional Indian sport that emphasizes speed, agility, and teamwork.
                                                  • \n
                                                • The game is played by two teams, each consisting of 12 players, but only 9 players are allowed to play actively during an innings.
                                                  • \n
                                                • It originated in Maharashtra, India, and has gained international recognition through tournaments and events.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Free Zones: \n
                                                  \n
                                                • The free zones are areas near the poles where chasers can change direction.
                                                  • \n
                                                • These zones are crucial for tactical gameplay and ensure smooth transitions for the chasing team.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Basic Rules of Kho-Kho: \n
                                                  \n
                                                • The game is divided into two innings, with each team taking turns as chasers and runners.
                                                  • \n
                                                • The primary objective of the chasing team is to tag all runners within the given time frame.
                                                  • \n
                                                • Runners aim to avoid being tagged by the chasers while staying within the designated playing area.

                                              • Statement 1: Bhutan's top trade partner is India, with trade primarily via maritime ports in West Bengal and Assam. \n
                                                  \n
                                                • This statement is incorrect. While India is Bhutan's primary trade partner, the trade between the two countries is largely conducted through land borders rather than maritime ports. Bhutan is a landlocked country with no access to maritime ports, and trade with India is facilitated through designated land border points such as Jaigaon (West Bengal) and others near Assam.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Statement 2: The meeting discussed automation, cross-border data exchange, and electronic cargo tracking. \n
                                                  \n
                                                • This statement is correct. The 6th India–Bhutan Joint Group of Customs (JGC) Meeting focused on enhancing trade facilitation measures. Key discussions included the adoption of automation systems, improving cross-border data exchange for smoother customs operations, and implementing electronic cargo tracking systems to ensure transparency and efficiency in trade.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Statement 3: India proposed capacity-building in Risk Management Systems and AEO certification for Bhutan. \n
                                                  \n
                                                • This statement is correct. During the meeting, India emphasized the importance of capacity-building initiatives for Bhutan's customs officials, particularly in areas such as Risk Management Systems (RMS) and Authorized Economic Operator (AEO) certification. These measures are aimed at strengthening Bhutan's customs framework and aligning it with international standards.
                                                  • \n

                                              \n\n

                                              Additional Information

                                              \n
                                                \n
                                              • India–Bhutan Joint Group of Customs (JGC): \n
                                                  \n
                                                • The JGC serves as a bilateral platform to discuss and resolve issues related to customs and trade facilitation between India and Bhutan.
                                                  • \n
                                                • It aims to enhance cooperation in areas such as customs clearance, tax policies, and trade regulations to promote seamless cross-border trade.
                                                  • \n
                                                • The meetings are held periodically to ensure that both countries remain aligned in their trade policies and practices.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Risk Management Systems (RMS): \n
                                                  \n
                                                • RMS is a mechanism implemented in customs operations to identify and prioritize high-risk consignments for inspection while facilitating the clearance of low-risk consignments.
                                                  • \n
                                                • It reduces transaction costs, ensures compliance, and improves the overall efficiency of customs processes.
                                                  • \n
                                                • India's proposal to assist Bhutan in implementing RMS reflects its commitment to strengthening Bhutan's trade infrastructure.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Authorized Economic Operator (AEO) Certification: \n
                                                  \n
                                                • AEO certification is an international standard that recognizes businesses as reliable and compliant trade partners.
                                                  • \n
                                                • It provides benefits such as faster customs clearance, reduced inspections, and priority treatment during customs procedures.
                                                  • \n
                                                • India's suggestion to assist Bhutan in adopting AEO certification aligns with the broader goal of facilitating trade and promoting economic collaboration.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Trade between India and Bhutan: \n
                                                  \n
                                                • India accounts for a significant portion of Bhutan's imports and exports, making it Bhutan's largest trading partner.
                                                  • \n
                                                • The trade primarily includes goods such as electricity, minerals, agricultural produce, and manufactured items.
                                                  • \n
                                                • The two nations also collaborate extensively in areas such as hydropower, infrastructure development, and capacity-building initiatives.
                                                  • \n
                                                \n
                                                • \n
                                              • Electronic Cargo Tracking Systems: \n
                                                  \n
                                                • These systems use technology such as GPS and RFID to monitor the movement of cargo in real-time.
                                                  • \n
                                                • Electronic tracking enhances transparency, reduces transit times, and ensures the safety and security of goods during transportation.
                                                  • \n
                                                • The adoption of such systems reflects the increasing emphasis on digitalization in trade and customs operations.

                                                    • राजपूतों ने मेवाड़, जयपुर, मारवाड़ और मालवा जैसे कई स्वतंत्र राज्य बनाए, जिन्होंने मध्यकालीन भारतीय राजनीति को आकार देने में महत्वपूर्ण भूमिका निभाई।

                                                          \n

                                                      The correct answer is Coastal Surveillance Radar System MoU.

                                                      \n\n

                                                      Key Points

                                                      \n\n
                                                        \n
                                                      • The Coastal Surveillance Radar System (CSRS) MoU is a key initiative under the India-Bangladesh defence cooperation, aimed at enhancing maritime domain awareness.
                                                        • \n
                                                      • This initiative involves the installation of advanced radar-based coastal surveillance systems along the Bangladeshi coastline to monitor and secure maritime regions.
                                                        • \n
                                                      • The CSRS supports both nations in combatting maritime threats such as illegal fishing, smuggling, piracy, and other illicit activities in shared waters.
                                                        • \n
                                                      • India has provided technical and logistical assistance to Bangladesh under this MoU, demonstrating its commitment to fostering regional maritime security and bilateral cooperation.
                                                        • \n
                                                      • The implementation of the system aligns with India’s broader vision of ensuring stability and security in the Indo-Pacific region.
                                                        • \n
                                                      \n\n

                                                      Additional Information

                                                      \n\n
                                                        \n
                                                      • India-Bangladesh Defence Relations:
                                                          \n
                                                        • India and Bangladesh have strengthened their defence partnership in recent years through joint exercises, training programs, and technology sharing.
                                                          • \n
                                                        • Key initiatives include the signing of defence MoUs, cooperation in counter-terrorism, and joint naval exercises like Bongosagar.
                                                          • \n
                                                        \n
                                                        • \n
                                                      • Maritime Domain Awareness (MDA):
                                                          \n
                                                        • MDA refers to the effective understanding of activities and threats in the maritime environment.
                                                          • \n
                                                        • The Coastal Surveillance Radar System is a tool that enhances MDA by providing real-time data on maritime traffic and potential threats.
                                                          • \n
                                                        \n
                                                        • \n
                                                      • Significance of the Indo-Pacific Region:
                                                          \n
                                                        • The Indo-Pacific is strategically significant as it hosts major sea routes, facilitating global trade and commerce.
                                                          • \n
                                                        • India’s focus on the region aligns with its Act East Policy and efforts to ensure freedom of navigation and regional stability.
                                                          • \n
                                                        \n
                                                        • \n
                                                      • Other Defence Initiatives:
                                                          \n
                                                        • Indo-Bangla Coordinated Patrols (CORPAT): These are joint patrols conducted to enhance security and combat transnational maritime crimes.
                                                          • \n
                                                        • Joint Training Programs: Indian armed forces provide training to Bangladeshi military personnel, fostering interoperability and mutual trust.
                                                          • \n
                                                        \n
                                                        • \n
                                                      \n\n

                                                      Important Points

                                                      \n\n
                                                        \n
                                                      • Comparison of Options:
                                                          \n
                                                        • Joint Bangladesh-India Naval Command Centre in Chattogram: No such command centre exists. This option is incorrect.
                                                          • \n
                                                        • Indo-Bangla Coastal Defence Protocol (ICDP): This is a fictitious term and is not a recognized initiative. Hence, incorrect.

                                                            • \n
                                                          • India has provided technical and logistical assistance to Bangladesh under this MoU, demonstrating its commitment to fostering regional maritime security and bilateral cooperation.

                                                          • The implementation of the system aligns with India’s broader vision of ensuring stability and security in the Indo-Pacific region.

                                                                        \n
                                                                      • 'द एडवेंचर्स ऑफ पद्मा एंड अ ब्लू डायनासोर' वैशाली श्रॉफ द्वारा लिखित एक आकर्षक बच्चों की किताब है। यह फंतासी और वैज्ञानिक तथ्यों के तत्वों को सहजता से जोड़ती है, जिससे यह बच्चों के लिए एक कल्पनाशील और शैक्षिक पठन बन जाती है।
                                                                        • \n
                                                                      • यह पुस्तक प्रागैतिहासिक जीवन और डायनासोर के विषय की पड़ताल करती है, जो युवा पाठकों के बीच पृथ्वी के प्राचीन इतिहास के बारे में जिज्ञासा जगाती है, जबकि इन अवधारणाओं को मजेदार और सुलभ तरीके से प्रस्तुत करती है।
                                                                        • \n
                                                                      • कहानी बच्चों को जीवाश्म विज्ञान, जीवाश्म और पृथ्वी के इतिहास जैसे वैज्ञानिक विषयों का पता लगाने के लिए प्रेरित करने के लिए डिज़ाइन की गई है, जबकि उन्हें रोमांच और फंतासी के मिश्रण के माध्यम से मनोरंजन भी प्रदान करती है।
                                                                        • \n
                                                                      • वैशाली श्रॉफ की कथा शैली का उद्देश्य युवा कल्पनाओं को उत्तेजित करना है, जबकि पाठकों को पृथ्वी के अतीत, प्राचीन जीवों और हमारी दुनिया को समझने में विज्ञान के महत्व से संबंधित विषयों पर सूक्ष्मता से शिक्षित करना है।
                                                                        • \n
                                                                      • पाठ्यपुस्तकों या विशुद्ध रूप से वैज्ञानिक कार्यों के विपरीत, यह पुस्तक मनोरंजन और शिक्षा के बीच के अंतर को पाटती है, जिससे यह शुरुआती पाठकों और युवा दिमागों के लिए आदर्श बन जाती है।

                                                                      The correct answer is Only 1.

                                                                      \n\n

                                                                      Key Points

                                                                      \n\n
                                                                        \n
                                                                      • Statement 1: The Deccan Trap region of the Peninsular Plateau is covered with black soil derived from igneous rocks. \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • The Deccan Trap is a large volcanic province in west-central India formed by extensive volcanic eruptions during the Cretaceous period.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • The rocks in this region, primarily basalt, weather over time to form fertile black soil, also known as regur soil.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • The black soil is highly suitable for growing crops like cotton, hence it is also called black cotton soil.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • Hence, this statement is correct.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n
                                                                        • \n\n
                                                                      • Statement 2: The Aravalli Hills are newly formed folded mountains stretching from Gujarat to Delhi. \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • The Aravalli Hills are among the oldest mountain ranges in the world, dating back to the Precambrian era (approximately 3.2 billion years ago).
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • They are not newly formed; rather, they are highly eroded and weathered over time due to their geological age.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • The range extends from Rajasthan and Haryana through parts of Gujarat to Delhi.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • Hence, this statement is incorrect.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n
                                                                        • \n
                                                                      \n\n

                                                                      Additional Information

                                                                      \n\n
                                                                        \n
                                                                      • Black Soil Characteristics: \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • Black soil is rich in calcium carbonate, magnesium, potash, and lime, making it highly fertile.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • It has a high water retention capacity, which is beneficial for crops like cotton, sugarcane, wheat, and oilseeds.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • This soil develops cracks during the dry season, which helps in better aeration.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n
                                                                        • \n\n
                                                                      • Formation of Deccan Trap: \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • The Deccan Traps were formed by successive volcanic eruptions that occurred about 65 million years ago.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • The eruptions resulted in the creation of a massive layer of igneous rocks that later weathered to form black soil.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n
                                                                        • \n\n
                                                                      • Aravalli Hills Features: \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • The Aravallis are composed of ancient crystalline rocks, such as granite, quartzite, and gneiss.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • They are considered relic mountains, significantly eroded over millions of years.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • The range is a critical source of minerals, including lead, zinc, and marble, and plays an important role in environmental conservation.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n
                                                                        • \n\n
                                                                      • Importance of Aravalli Range: \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • The Aravalli Hills act as a natural barrier against the spread of the Thar Desert into the eastern parts of India.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • They are home to a variety of flora and fauna and serve as a crucial ecological zone in the region.
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n

                                                                        • पी. आर. श्रीजेश की उल्लेखनीय उपलब्धियां: \n
                                                                            \n
                                                                          • उन्होंने 2014 एशियाई खेलों में भारत की जीत में महत्वपूर्ण भूमिका निभाई, जहाँ उन्होंने स्वर्ण पदक जीता।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • श्रीजेश ने कई विश्व कप और चैंपियंस ट्रॉफी टूर्नामेंटों में भारत का प्रतिनिधित्व किया है।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • उन्हें अर्जुन पुरस्कार (2015) और खेल रत्न पुरस्कार (2021) से सम्मानित किया गया है।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • विकल्पों में अन्य खेल हस्तियां: \n
                                                                            \n
                                                                          • धनराज पिल्ले: पूर्व भारतीय हॉकी खिलाड़ी और कप्तान, अपने असाधारण कौशल और भारतीय हॉकी में योगदान के लिए जाने जाते हैं, लेकिन उन्हें 2025 में पद्म भूषण नहीं मिला।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • मैरी कॉम: एक भारतीय मुक्केबाज और छह बार की विश्व चैंपियन, मैरी कॉम को 2013 में पद्म भूषण मिला है, लेकिन 2025 में नहीं।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • विराट कोहली: एक भारतीय क्रिकेटर और भारतीय क्रिकेट टीम के पूर्व कप्तान, विराट को राजीव गांधी खेल रत्न पुरस्कार सहित कई सम्मान मिले हैं, लेकिन उन्हें 2025 में पद्म भूषण नहीं मिला।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n60:T1e1d,\n

                                                                        ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍।

                                                                        \n

                                                                        Key Points

                                                                        \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • ଜଣେ ଭାରତୀୟ ଫିଲ୍ଡ ହକି ଖେଳାଳି ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍, ଖେଳ ପ୍ରତି ତାଙ୍କର ଅସାଧାରଣ ଅବଦାନ ପାଇଁ 2025 ରେ ପ୍ରତିଷ୍ଠିତ ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣରେ ସମ୍ମାନିତ ହୋଇଥିଲେ।
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ ଭାରତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ ହକି ଦଳ ପାଇଁ ଗୋଲକିପର ଭାବରେ ଜଣେ ପ୍ରମୁଖ ପ୍ରଦର୍ଶନକାରୀ ଥିଲେ ଏବଂ ଅନେକ ଆନ୍ତର୍ଜାତୀୟ ଟୁର୍ଣ୍ଣାମେଣ୍ଟରେ ଏକ ଗୁରୁତ୍ୱପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଭୂମିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ।
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ଟୋକିଓ 2020 ଅଲିମ୍ପିକ୍ସରେ ଭାରତକୁ କାଂସ୍ୟ ପଦକ ଜିତାଇବାରେ ସେ ପ୍ରମୁଖ ଭୂମିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ, ଯାହା ଅଲିମ୍ପିକ୍ସରେ ହକିରେ 41 ବର୍ଷର ପଦକ ଶୁଖିଲା ପଡ଼ିଥିଲା।
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ଜଣେ ଅଭିଜ୍ଞ ଖେଳାଳି ଭାବରେ, ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ ଭାରତୀୟ ଦଳର ନେତୃତ୍ୱ ନେଇଥିଲେ ଏବଂ ଏସୀୟ ଚାମ୍ପିଅନ୍ସ ଟ୍ରଫି ଏବଂ ଏସିଆ କପ୍ ଭଳି ଟୁର୍ଣ୍ଣାମେଣ୍ଟରେ ବିଜୟରେ ସହଯୋଗ କରିଥିଲେ।
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣ ହେଉଛି ଭାରତର ତୃତୀୟ ସର୍ବୋଚ୍ଚ ନାଗରିକ ପୁରସ୍କାର, ଯାହା କ୍ରୀଡ଼ା ସମେତ ଯେକୌଣସି କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଉଚ୍ଚକୋଟୀର ବିଶିଷ୍ଟ ସେବାକୁ ସ୍ୱୀକୃତି ଦେଇଥାଏ।
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        \n

                                                                        Additional Information

                                                                        \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                        • ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍:\n
                                                                            \n
                                                                          • ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ 1988 ମସିହା ମେ 8 ରେ କେରଳର କିଝାକ୍କାମ୍ବଲମ୍ ରେ ଜନ୍ମଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ସେ 2006 ରେ ଭାରତୀୟ ଜାତୀୟ ହକି ଦଳରେ ଯୋଗ ଦେଇଥିଲେ ଏବଂ ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ଏହାର ସବୁଠାରୁ ସ୍ଥିର ପ୍ରଦର୍ଶନକାରୀମାନଙ୍କ ମଧ୍ୟରୁ ଜଣେ ଅଟନ୍ତି।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ସେ 2021 ରେ ୱାର୍ଲ୍ଡ ଗେମ୍ସ ଆଥଲେଟ୍ ଅଫ୍ ଦି ଇଅର ଏବଂ ଅନେକ ଥର FIH ଗୋଲକିପର ଅଫ୍ ଦି ଇଅର ପୁରସ୍କାର ସହିତ ଅନେକ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତିଗତ ପୁରସ୍କାର ଜିତିଛନ୍ତି।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ସେ ତାଙ୍କର ଦ୍ରୁତ ପ୍ରତିକ୍ରିୟା, ତୀକ୍ଷ୍ଣ ନିଷ୍ପତ୍ତି ନେବା ଏବଂ ପଡିଆରେ ନେତୃତ୍ୱ ନେବାର କ୍ଷମତା ପାଇଁ ଜଣାଶୁଣା।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣ ପୁରସ୍କାର:\n
                                                                            \n
                                                                          • ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣ 1954 ରେ ପ୍ରତିଷ୍ଠିତ ହୋଇଥିଲା ଏବଂ ଏହା ଭାରତର ସର୍ବୋଚ୍ଚ ନାଗରିକ ପୁରସ୍କାର ମଧ୍ୟରୁ ଅନ୍ୟତମ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ଏହା ପ୍ରତିବର୍ଷ ଗଣତନ୍ତ୍ର ଦିବସରେ ପ୍ରଦାନ କରାଯାଏ ଏବଂ କ୍ରୀଡ଼ା, କଳା, ବିଜ୍ଞାନ ଏବଂ ସାଧାରଣ ବ୍ୟାପାର ସମେତ ବିଭିନ୍ନ କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଉଚ୍ଚକୋଟୀର ବିଶିଷ୍ଟ ସେବା ପାଇଁ ଦିଆଯାଏ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ପୂର୍ବରୁ ଏହି ପୁରସ୍କାର ପାଇଥିବା କ୍ରୀଡ଼ା ବ୍ୟକ୍ତିତ୍ୱମାନଙ୍କ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ମେରୀ କମ୍, ଲିଆଣ୍ଡର୍ ପେସ୍, ଏବଂ ପି. ଭି. ସିନ୍ଧୁ ଅନ୍ତର୍ଭୁକ୍ତ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ ଙ୍କର ଉଲ୍ଲେଖନୀୟ ସଫଳତା:\n
                                                                            \n
                                                                          • 2014 ଏସୀୟ ଗେମ୍ସରେ ଭାରତର ବିଜୟରେ ସେ ଏକ ଗୁରୁତ୍ୱପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଭୂମିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ, ଯେଉଁଠାରେ ସେମାନେ ସ୍ୱର୍ଣ୍ଣ ପଦକ ଜିତିଥିଲେ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ ଅନେକ ବିଶ୍ୱକପ୍ ଏବଂ ଚାମ୍ପିଅନ୍ସ ଟ୍ରଫି ଟୁର୍ଣ୍ଣାମେଣ୍ଟରେ ଭାରତକୁ ପ୍ରତିନିଧିତ୍ୱ କରିଛନ୍ତି।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ସେ ଅର୍ଜୁନ ପୁରସ୍କାର (2015) ଏବଂ ଖେଳ ରତ୍ନ ପୁରସ୍କାର (2021)ରେ ସମ୍ମାନିତ ହୋଇଛନ୍ତି।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          \n
                                                                          • \n
                                                                        • ବିକଳ୍ପରେ ଥିବା ଅନ୍ୟ କ୍ରୀଡ଼ା ବ୍ୟକ୍ତିତ୍ୱ:\n
                                                                            \n
                                                                          • ଧନରାଜ ପିଲାଇ: ପୂର୍ବତନ ଭାରତୀୟ ହକି ଖେଳାଳି ଏବଂ ଅଧିନାୟକ, ତାଙ୍କର ଅସାଧାରଣ କୌଶଳ ଏବଂ ଭାରତୀୟ ହକିରେ ଅବଦାନ ପାଇଁ ଜଣାଶୁଣା, କିନ୍ତୁ ସେ 2025 ରେ ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣ ପାଇନଥିଲେ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ମେରୀ କମ୍: ଜଣେ ଭାରତୀୟ ବକ୍ସର ଏବଂ ଛଅ ଥରର ବିଶ୍ୱ ଚାମ୍ପିଅନ୍, ମେରୀ କମ୍ 2013 ରେ ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣ ପାଇଛନ୍ତି, କିନ୍ତୁ 2025 ରେ ନୁହେଁ।
                                                                            • \n
                                                                          • ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍।

                                                                            \n

                                                                            Key Points

                                                                            \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • ଜଣେ ଭାରତୀୟ ଫିଲ୍ଡ ହକି ଖେଳାଳି ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍, ଖେଳ ପ୍ରତି ତାଙ୍କର ଅସାଧାରଣ ଅବଦାନ ପାଇଁ 2025 ରେ ପ୍ରତିଷ୍ଠିତ ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣରେ ସମ୍ମାନିତ ହୋଇଥିଲେ।
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ ଭାରତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ ହକି ଦଳ ପାଇଁ ଗୋଲକିପର ଭାବରେ ଜଣେ ପ୍ରମୁଖ ପ୍ରଦର୍ଶନକାରୀ ଥିଲେ ଏବଂ ଅନେକ ଆନ୍ତର୍ଜାତୀୟ ଟୁର୍ଣ୍ଣାମେଣ୍ଟରେ ଏକ ଗୁରୁତ୍ୱପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଭୂମିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ।
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • ଟୋକିଓ 2020 ଅଲିମ୍ପିକ୍ସରେ ଭାରତକୁ କାଂସ୍ୟ ପଦକ ଜିତାଇବାରେ ସେ ପ୍ରମୁଖ ଭୂମିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ, ଯାହା ଅଲିମ୍ପିକ୍ସରେ ହକିରେ 41 ବର୍ଷର ପଦକ ଶୁଖିଲା ପଡ଼ିଥିଲା।
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • ଜଣେ ଅଭିଜ୍ଞ ଖେଳାଳି ଭାବରେ, ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ ଭାରତୀୟ ଦଳର ନେତୃତ୍ୱ ନେଇଥିଲେ ଏବଂ ଏସୀୟ ଚାମ୍ପିଅନ୍ସ ଟ୍ରଫି ଏବଂ ଏସିଆ କପ୍ ଭଳି ଟୁର୍ଣ୍ଣାମେଣ୍ଟରେ ବିଜୟରେ ସହଯୋଗ କରିଥିଲେ।
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • ପଦ୍ମ ଭୂଷଣ ହେଉଛି ଭାରତର ତୃତୀୟ ସର୍ବୋଚ୍ଚ ନାଗରିକ ପୁରସ୍କାର, ଯାହା କ୍ରୀଡ଼ା ସମେତ ଯେକୌଣସି କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ ଉଚ୍ଚକୋଟୀର ବିଶିଷ୍ଟ ସେବାକୁ ସ୍ୱୀକୃତି ଦେଇଥାଏ।
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            \n

                                                                            Additional Information

                                                                            \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • ପି. ଆର୍. ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍:\n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • ଶ୍ରୀଜେଶ୍ 1988 ମସିହା ମେ 8 ରେ କେରଳର କିଝାକ୍କାମ୍ବଲମ୍ ରେ ଜନ୍ମଗ୍ରହଣ କରିଥିଲେ।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • ସେ 2006 ରେ ଭାରତୀୟ ଜାତୀୟ ହକି ଦଳରେ ଯୋଗ ଦେଇଥିଲେ ଏବଂ ସେହି ଦିନଠାରୁ ଏହାର ସବୁଠାରୁ ସ୍ଥିର ପ୍ରଦର୍ଶନକାରୀମାନଙ୍କ ମଧ୍ୟରୁ ଜଣେ ଅଟନ୍ତି।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • ସେ 2021 ରେ ୱାର୍ଲ୍ଡ ଗେମ୍ସ ଆଥଲେଟ୍ ଅଫ୍ ଦି ଇଅର ଏବଂ ଅନେକ ଥର FIH ଗୋଲକିପର ଅଫ୍ ଦି ଇଅର ପୁରସ୍କାର ସହିତ ଅନେକ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତିଗତ ପୁରସ୍କାର ଜିତିଛନ୍ତି।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • ସେ ତାଙ୍କର ଦ୍ରୁତ ପ୍ରତିକ୍ରିୟା, ତୀକ୍ଷ୍ଣ ନିଷ୍ପତ୍ତି ନେବା ଏବଂ ପଡିଆରେ ନେତୃତ୍ୱ ନେବାର କ୍ଷମତା ପାଇଁ ଜଣାଶୁଣା।

                                                                                • \n

                                                                                • \n

                                                                            The correct answer is All 1, 2, and 3 are correct.

                                                                            \n

                                                                            Key Points

                                                                            \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • India's Green Credit Programme is designed to incentivize individuals, institutions, and industries to undertake voluntary environmental actions such as afforestation, water harvesting, waste management, and energy efficiency measures. Hence, statement 1 is correct.
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • The programme operates under the Environment (Protection) Act, 1986, which is a key legislation for environmental protection in India. It provides the legal framework for the programme's implementation. Hence, statement 2 is correct.
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • Credits earned through the programme are tradable, meaning they can be bought and sold in the market. These credits are recognized by financial institutions, enabling participants to monetize their environmental efforts. Hence, statement 3 is correct.
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • The Green Credit Programme represents a significant step towards sustainable development by aligning economic incentives with environmental conservation.
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • Tradable credits allow industries and businesses to offset their environmental footprints by investing in green initiatives.
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            \n

                                                                            Additional Information

                                                                            \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • Environment (Protection) Act, 1986:
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • This act was enacted in 1986 following the Bhopal gas tragedy to provide measures for the protection and improvement of the environment.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • It empowers the central government to take all necessary measures to protect the environment and prevent pollution.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The act is comprehensive, covering aspects such as emissions, hazardous waste management, and environmental standards.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • Green Credit Trading Mechanism:
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • Trading allows entities that perform better environmentally to sell their credits to those that fall short, thus encouraging widespread adoption of green practices.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • Examples include carbon credits, which are similar in concept but focus specifically on reducing greenhouse gas emissions.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs):
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • The Green Credit Programme aligns with several SDGs, including SDG 13 (Climate Action) and SDG 15 (Life on Land), by promoting afforestation and reducing environmental degradation.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • It also indirectly supports SDG 8 (Decent Work and Economic Growth) by creating economic opportunities through green initiatives.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • Global Examples of Green Credit Systems:
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • Countries like China have implemented similar programmes, such as the China Certified Emissions Reduction (CCER) system.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The European Union operates the Emissions Trading System (EU ETS), which is one of the largest carbon trading platforms globally.
                                                                                • \n

                                                                            \n\n

                                                                            Key Points

                                                                            \n\n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • Statement 1: The Deccan Trap region of the Peninsular Plateau is covered with black soil derived from igneous rocks. \n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • The Deccan Trap is a large volcanic province in west-central India formed by extensive volcanic eruptions during the Cretaceous period.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The rocks in this region, primarily basalt, weather over time to form fertile black soil, also known as regur soil.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The black soil is highly suitable for growing crops like cotton, hence it is also called black cotton soil.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • Hence, this statement is correct.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n
                                                                            • Statement 2: The Aravalli Hills are newly formed folded mountains stretching from Gujarat to Delhi. \n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • The Aravalli Hills are among the oldest mountain ranges in the world, dating back to the Precambrian era (approximately 3.2 billion years ago).
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • They are not newly formed; rather, they are highly eroded and weathered over time due to their geological age.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The range extends from Rajasthan and Haryana through parts of Gujarat to Delhi.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • Hence, this statement is incorrect.
                                                                                • \n

                                                                            Additional Information

                                                                            \n\n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • Black Soil Characteristics: \n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • Black soil is rich in calcium carbonate, magnesium, potash, and lime, making it highly fertile.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • It has a high water retention capacity, which is beneficial for crops like cotton, sugarcane, wheat, and oilseeds.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • This soil develops cracks during the dry season, which helps in better aeration.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n
                                                                            • Formation of Deccan Trap: \n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • The Deccan Traps were formed by successive volcanic eruptions that occurred about 65 million years ago.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The eruptions resulted in the creation of a massive layer of igneous rocks that later weathered to form black soil.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n
                                                                            • Aravalli Hills Features: \n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • The Aravallis are composed of ancient crystalline rocks, such as granite, quartzite, and gneiss.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • They are considered relic mountains, significantly eroded over millions of years.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • The range is a critical source of minerals, including lead, zinc, and marble, and plays an important role in environmental conservation.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n
                                                                            • Importance of Aravalli Range: \n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • The Aravalli Hills act as a natural barrier against the spread of the Thar Desert into the eastern parts of India.
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • They are home to a variety of flora and fauna and serve as a crucial ecological zone in the region.
                                                                                • \n

                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • The Aravallis have a significant role in influencing the climate of the region by blocking hot desert winds.
                                                                              • \n
                                                                            • Black soil is found predominantly in the Deccan Plateau states such as Maharashtra, Gujarat, and Madhya Pradesh.

                                                                            Key Points

                                                                            \n\n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • कथन 1: प्रायद्वीपीय पठार का दक्कन ट्रैप क्षेत्र आग्नेय चट्टानों से प्राप्त काली मिट्टी से ढका है।\n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • दक्कन ट्रैप पश्चिम-मध्य भारत में एक बड़ा ज्वालामुखी प्रांत है जो क्रेतेशस काल के दौरान व्यापक ज्वालामुखी विस्फोटों से बना था।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • इस क्षेत्र की चट्टानें, मुख्य रूप से बेसाल्ट, समय के साथ अपक्षयित होकर उपजाऊ काली मिट्टी बनाती हैं, जिसे रेगुर मिट्टी भी कहा जाता है।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • काली मिट्टी कपास जैसी फसलों को उगाने के लिए अत्यधिक उपयुक्त है, इसलिए इसे काली कपासी मिट्टी भी कहा जाता है।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • अतः, यह कथन सही है।

                                                                              • \n\n

                                                                                \n
                                                                              • अरावली पहाड़ियाँ दुनिया की सबसे पुरानी पर्वत शृंखलाओं में से हैं, जो प्रीकैम्ब्रियन युग (लगभग 3.2 अरब वर्ष पहले) की हैं।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • ये नई बनी हुई नहीं हैं; बल्कि, ये अपनी भूगर्भीय आयु के कारण समय के साथ अत्यधिक अपरदित और अपक्षयित हो चुकी हैं।
                                                                                • \n

                                                                            Additional Information

                                                                            \n\n
                                                                              \n
                                                                            • काली मिट्टी की विशेषताएँ:\n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • काली मिट्टी कैल्शियम कार्बोनेट, मैग्नीशियम, पोटाश और चूने से भरपूर होती है, जो इसे अत्यधिक उपजाऊ बनाती है।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • इसमें उच्च जल धारण क्षमता होती है, जो कपास, गन्ना, गेहूँ और तिलहन जैसी फसलों के लिए फायदेमंद है।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • यह मिट्टी शुष्क मौसम के दौरान दरारें विकसित करती है, जो बेहतर वातन में मदद करती है।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n
                                                                            • दक्कन ट्रैप का निर्माण:\n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • दक्कन ट्रैप का निर्माण लगभग 65 मिलियन वर्ष पहले हुए लगातार ज्वालामुखी विस्फोटों से हुआ था।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • विस्फोटों के परिणामस्वरूप आग्नेय चट्टानों की एक विशाल परत का निर्माण हुआ, जो बाद में काली मिट्टी बनाने के लिए अपक्षयित हो गई।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n
                                                                            • अरावली पहाड़ियों की विशेषताएँ:\n
                                                                                \n
                                                                              • अरावली प्राचीन क्रिस्टलीय चट्टानों, जैसे ग्रेनाइट, क्वार्टजाइट और गनीस से बनी हैं।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • इन्हें अवशिष्ट पर्वत माना जाता है, जो लाखों वर्षों में काफी अपरदित हो चुकी हैं।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              • यह शृंखला सीसा, जस्ता और संगमरमर सहित खनिजों का एक महत्वपूर्ण स्रोत है, और पर्यावरण संरक्षण में महत्वपूर्ण भूमिका निभाती है।
                                                                                • \n
                                                                              \n
                                                                              • \n\n

                                                                                        \n

                                                                                        The correct answer is Only 1.

                                                                                        \n\n

                                                                                        Key Points

                                                                                        \n\n
                                                                                          \n
                                                                                        • Statement 1: A significant number of adolescent girls in India suffer from anaemia. \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                          • This statement is correct. According to the National Family Health Survey (NFHS-5, 2019-21), about 59.1% of adolescent girls aged 15-19 years in India suffer from anaemia.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          • Anaemia in adolescent girls is primarily caused by iron deficiency, poor dietary habits, and inadequate access to nutrition.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          • It is a critical public health issue that affects growth, cognitive development, and overall well-being.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          • Government initiatives like the Weekly Iron and Folic Acid Supplementation (WIFS) program aim to address this issue.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          \n
                                                                                          • \n\n
                                                                                        • Statement 2: The diet available to adolescents in India is generally rich in all nutrients. \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                          • This statement is incorrect. The diet available to many adolescents in India is often inadequate in essential nutrients like iron, protein, and vitamins.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          • Many adolescents, especially in rural and economically weaker sections, face nutrition insecurity due to poverty, lack of awareness, and limited access to diverse foods.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          • Staple diets in India are often carbohydrate-rich but lack sufficient micronutrients like iron, zinc, and vitamin B12, which are essential for healthy development.
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          \n सही उत्तर केवल 1 है।

                                                                                          \n\n

                                                                                          Key Points

                                                                                          \n\n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                          • कथन 1: भारत में बड़ी संख्या में किशोरियों में एनीमिया है। \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • यह कथन सही है। राष्ट्रीय परिवार स्वास्थ्य सर्वेक्षण (NFHS-5, 2019-21) के अनुसार, भारत में 15-19 वर्ष की लगभग 59.1% किशोरियाँ एनीमिया से पीड़ित हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • किशोरियों में एनीमिया मुख्य रूप से आयरन की कमी, खराब आहार संबंधी आदतों और पोषण तक अपर्याप्त पहुंच के कारण होता है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • यह एक महत्वपूर्ण सार्वजनिक स्वास्थ्य मुद्दा है जो वृद्धि, संज्ञानात्मक विकास और समग्र कल्याण को प्रभावित करता है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • साप्ताहिक आयरन और फोलिक एसिड अनुपूरण (WIFS) कार्यक्रम जैसी सरकारी पहल का उद्देश्य इस मुद्दे का समाधान करना है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n\n
                                                                                          • कथन 2: भारत में किशोरों के लिए उपलब्ध आहार आमतौर पर सभी पोषक तत्वों से भरपूर होता है। \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • यह कथन गलत है। भारत में कई किशोरों के लिए उपलब्ध आहार में अक्सर आयरन, प्रोटीन और विटामिन जैसे आवश्यक पोषक तत्वों की कमी होती है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • कई किशोर, विशेष रूप से ग्रामीण और आर्थिक रूप से कमजोर वर्गों में, गरीबी, जागरूकता की कमी और विविध खाद्य पदार्थों तक सीमित पहुंच के कारण पोषण असुरक्षा का सामना करते हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • भारत में मुख्य आहार अक्सर कार्बोहाइड्रेट से भरपूर होते हैं, लेकिन उनमें आयरन, जिंक और विटामिन B12 जैसे पर्याप्त सूक्ष्म पोषक तत्व नहीं होते हैं, जो स्वस्थ विकास के लिए आवश्यक हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          \n\n

                                                                                          Additional Information

                                                                                          \n\n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                          • भारत में एनीमिया: \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • एनीमिया एक ऐसी स्थिति है जिसमें रक्त में हीमोग्लोबिन का स्तर कम होता है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • यह भारत में एक महत्वपूर्ण सार्वजनिक स्वास्थ्य चुनौती है, जो बच्चों, किशोरों और प्रजनन आयु की महिलाओं को प्रभावित करती है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • एनीमिया का मुख्य कारण आयरन की कमी है, लेकिन फोलिक एसिड की कमी, संक्रमण (उदाहरण के लिए, मलेरिया) और आनुवंशिक विकार (उदाहरण के लिए, थैलेसीमिया) जैसे अन्य कारक भी इसमें योगदान करते हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n\n
                                                                                          • एनीमिया से निपटने के लिए सरकारी पहल: \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • राष्ट्रीय आयरन प्लस पहल (NIPI) बच्चों, किशोरों और गर्भवती महिलाओं को आयरन और फोलिक एसिड अनुपूरण प्रदान करने के लिए शुरू की गई थी।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • पोषण अभियान (राष्ट्रीय पोषण मिशन) का उद्देश्य पोषण संबंधी प्रथाओं में सुधार करके महिलाओं और बच्चों में एनीमिया को कम करना है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • मध्याह्न भोजन योजना और एकीकृत बाल विकास सेवाएं (ICDS) भी बच्चों और किशोरों के बीच पोषण में सुधार पर ध्यान केंद्रित करती हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n\n
                                                                                          • किशोरों में आहार प्रतिरूप: \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • भारतीय किशोर अक्सर कार्बोहाइड्रेट (जैसे चावल, गेहूं) से भरपूर, लेकिन आयरन, कैल्शियम और विटामिन जैसे प्रोटीन और सूक्ष्म पोषक तत्वों में कम आहार का सेवन करते हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • गरीबी, सामाजिक-सांस्कृतिक प्रथाएं और सीमित आहार विविधता जैसे कारक खराब पोषण में योगदान करते हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • फास्ट फूड और प्रसंस्कृत खाद्य पदार्थों के सेवन से पोषण संबंधी विकारों, जिनमें मोटापा और कमी शामिल है, में भी वृद्धि हुई है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n\n
                                                                                          • किशोर पोषण का महत्व: \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • किशोरावस्था शारीरिक वृद्धि और संज्ञानात्मक विकास के लिए एक महत्वपूर्ण अवधि है। इस चरण के दौरान उचित पोषण एक स्वस्थ वयस्क आबादी के निर्माण के लिए आवश्यक है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • किशोरावस्था के दौरान प्रमुख पोषक तत्वों की कमी से रुका हुआ विकास, खराब शैक्षणिक प्रदर्शन और संक्रमणों के प्रति संवेदनशीलता बढ़ सकती है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • राष्ट्रीय किशोर स्वास्थ्य कार्यक्रम (RKSK) जैसे कार्यक्रम किशोरों की स्वास्थ्य और पोषण संबंधी आवश्यकताओं को पूरा करने पर ध्यान केंद्रित करते हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n
                                                                                          \n\n

                                                                                          Important Points

                                                                                          \n\n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                          • NFHS-5 से प्रमुख डेटा: \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • 59.1% किशोरियाँ (15-19 वर्ष) एनीमिया से ग्रसित हैं, जो आयरन और आवश्यक पोषक तत्वों में व्यापक कमी को दर्शाता है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • पौष्टिक भोजन तक सीमित पहुंच के कारण शहरी क्षेत्रों की तुलना में ग्रामीण क्षेत्रों में इसकी व्यापकता अधिक है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n\n
                                                                                          • एनीमिया के कारण: \n
                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • अपर्याप्त आहार सेवन: आयरन युक्त खाद्य पदार्थों जैसे हरी पत्तेदार सब्जियों, दालों और मांस की कमी वाले आहार।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • कुअवशोषण संबंधी समस्याएं: आंतों के संक्रमण जैसी स्थितियां जो पोषक तत्वों के उचित अवशोषण को रोकती हैं।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • मासिक धर्म रक्त की हानि: किशोरियों को अक्सर मासिक धर्म के दौरान आयरन की हानि होती है, जिससे एनीमिया का खतरा बढ़ जाता है।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            \n
                                                                                            • \n\n

                                                                                              \n
                                                                                            • पालक, गुड़ और दालों जैसे आयरन युक्त खाद्य पदार्थों के सेवन को बढ़ावा देना।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • संतुलित आहार के महत्व पर शिक्षा अभियानों के माध्यम से जागरूकता बढ़ाना।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • प्रोटीन, विटामिन और स्वस्थ वसा सहित आहार विविधता को प्रोत्साहित करना।
                                                                                              • \n
                                                                                            • नियमित स्वास्थ्य जांच लागू करना और आयरन-फोलिक एसिड अनुपूरक प्रदान करना।
                                                                                              • \n
